1a1(dkar chag): @#//bka' 'gyur rin po che'i mtshan tho bzhugs so//
1b1(dkar chag): @##/oM swasti sid+d+haM/rgyal mchog nyi ma'i gnyen gyi !bka' 'gyur ro cog shel dkar mi 'gyur rdo rje'i rdzong 
1b2(dkar chag): du par du bsgrub pa'i chos tshan go rim gyi tho dkar chag tu bkod par/'dul ba'i gras/ka par rab byung gi gzhi/dgag dbye 
1b3(dkar chag): dbyar gnas/ko lpags kyi gzhi'i stod cha/kha par ko lpags kyi gzhi'i smad cha dang /sman gzhi'i stod cha yan/ga 
1b4(dkar chag): par sman gzhi'i smad cha/gos/sra brgyang /kau sham+b+hi/las kyi/dmar ser can/gang zag/spo ba'i/gso sbyong bzhag pa'i 
1b5(dkar chag): gnas mal/rtsod pa'i/dbyen gyi gzhi'i stod cha/nga par dbyen gyi gzhi'i smad cha/ca par dge slong pha'i so thar/
2a1(dkar chag): @##//chabar lhag ma bcu'i smad cha/jabar ltung byed bdun cu rtsa gnyis pa/nyabar ltung byed rdzogs pa/tapar 
2a2(dkar chag): dge slong ma'i so thar dang /de'i rgyas bshad dge slong ma'i rnam 'byed/thabar phran tshegs bam po nyer bdun yan/dabar phran tshegs bam 
2a3(dkar chag): bo nyer drug nas bam po lnga bcu rtsa dgra rdzogs pa yan/nabar gzhung bla ma zhu ba ma rdzogs pa'i bam po bcu gnyis dang /zhu ba rdzogs pa bam po nyer 
2a4(dkar chag): gnyis yan/pabar zhu ba rdzogs pa'i bam po nyer gsum nas/bam po lnga bcu rtsa gsum rdzogs pa4na////'khum gyi gras/
2a5(dkar chag): 5/par bam po dang po nas nyer bzhi bar/khapar nyer lnga nas lnga bcu rtsa gcig bar/gapar lnga bcu rtsa gnyis nas bdun cu rtsa brgyad par/ngapar 
2a6(dkar chag): bdun cu rtsa dgu nas brgya gsum pa'i bar/cabar brgya bzhi pa nas brgya nyer dgu bar/chabar brgya sum cu pa nas brgya rang gsum bar/japar 
2b1(dkar chag): brgya nga bzhi nas brgya don lnga bar/nya bar brgya don drug nas nyis brgya gnyis pa'i bar/ta par nyis brgya gsum pa nas nyis brgya nyer brgyad bar/
2b2(dkar chag): tha par nyis brgya nyer dgu nas nyis brgya nga bzhi bar/da bar nyis brgya nga lnga nas nyis brgya don bdun bar/na par nyis brgya don brgyad nas sum 
2b3(dkar chag): brgya tham pa rdzogs pa yan////nyi khri ka par bam po dang po nas nyer bdun bar/kha par nyer brgyad nas nga gnyis bar/ga 
2b4(dkar chag): par nga gsum nas brgyad cu rtsa gsum rdzogs pa yan////khri brgyad ka par bam po dang po na nyer lnga bar/kha par nyer drug nas lnga bcu 
2b5(dkar chag): bam pa'i bar/ga par nga gcig nas drug cu rdzogs pa yan////shes khri ka par bam po so bzhi rdzogs pa yan////brgyad stong ka 
2b6(dkar chag): par bam po nyer bzhi rdzogs pa yan///khri sna tshogs ka par rab rtsal rnam gnon gyis zhus pa bam po so bdun/shar phyin gras bdun brgya pa 
3a1(dkar chag): @#//lnga brgya pa/sdud pa/rdo rje gcod pa/tshul brgya lnga bcu pa/mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad pa/lnga bcu pa/yi ge gcig ma/kau shi ka/
3a2(dkar chag): yi ge nyung ngu/sgo nyi shu lnga pa/shes rab snying po/nyi ma'i snying po/zla ba'i snying po/kun tu bzang po/lag na rdo rje/rdo rje rgyal mtshan bcas rdzogs pa 
3a3(dkar chag): yin////dkon brtsegs bral/ka/par sdom pa gsum bstan pa/sgo mtha' yas pa rnam par sbyong ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba bsam gyis 
3a4(dkar chag): mi khyab pa bstan pa/rmi lam brtan pa/'od dpag med kyi zhing gi bkod pa/kha par de bzhin gshegs pa mi 'khrugs pa'i bkod pa/go cha'i bkod pa 
3a5(dkar chag): bstan pa'i mdo//chos kyi dbyings rang bzhin dbyer med par bstan pa/chos bcu pa'i mdo//kun nas sgo'i le'u/'od zer kun tu bkye ba bstan pa/
3a6(dkar chag): ga par byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod/dga' bo mngal du 'jug pa bstan pa/dga' bo mngal na gnas pa bstan pa/da par 'phags pa 'jam dpal 
3b1(dkar chag): gyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa/yab sras mjal ba'i mdo//gang bas zhus pa/yul 'khor skyong gis zhus pa/rtsa bar khyim bdag drag shul can gyis zhus pa/glog thob 
3b2(dkar chag): gyis zhus pa mi zad pa'i gter bstan pa'i le'u/sgyu ma mkhan bzang po lung bstan pa/cho 'phrul chen po bstan pa lha'i bu ded dpon gyis zhus pa/'phags pa byams pa'i seng ge'i sgra chen 
3b3(dkar chag): po|'dul ba rnam par bstan la dbab pa nye bar 'khor gyis zhus pa/lhag pa'i bsam pa bskul ba'i mdo//lag bzang gis zhus pa/khyim bdag dpal byin gyis zhus pa/bar ma la'i 
3b4(dkar chag): rgyal po 'char byed kyis zhus pa/bu mo blo gros bzang mos zhus pa/bu mo gang+gu'i mchog gis zhus pa/mya ngan med kyi byin pa lung bstan pa bu mo dri ma med pa'i byin pas zhus pa/
3b5(dkar chag): yon tan rin chen me tog kun tu rgyas pas zhus pa/sangs rgyas kyi yul bsam gyis mi khyab par bstan pa/cha par lha'i bu blo gros rab gnas kyis zhus pa/seng ges zhus pa/
3b6(dkar chag): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang chen thabs la mkhas pa byang chub sems dpa' ye shes dam pas zhus pa/tshong dpon bzang skyong gis zhus pa/bu mo rnam dag dang bas zhus pa/'phags pa 
3b7(dkar chag): byams pas zhus pa/byams zhus chos brgyad pa/'od srung gis zhus pa/rin po che'i phung po blo gros mi zad pas zhus pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bdun brgya pa/gtsug na 
4a1(dkar chag): @#//rin chen gyis zhus pa/lha mo dpal bdeng sang gaz+ra'i le'u/drang srong rgyas pa bzhugs pa bcas rdzogs pa yan////bal chen gras/ka 
4a2(dkar chag): par 'jig rten gyis dbang po thams cad kyi rgyan gyi le'u/de bzhin gshegs pa'i le'u/kun tu bzang po'i ting 'dzin/rnam par 'phrul gyi le'u/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 
4a3(dkar chag): bstan pa'i le'u/gzhi dang snying po me tog gi brgyan pa'i yon tan gyi le'u/'jig rten gyi kha mas rgya mtsho'i 'khor yug gi rgyan pa'i le'u/sa gzhi'i rgyan bstan pa'i le'u/
4a4(dkar chag): zhing gi rgyud kyi gnas bstan pa'i le'u/'jig rten khams kyi rgyud dgod pa'i le'u/rnam par snang mdzad kyi le'u/de bzhin gshegs ba phal po che'i le'u/sangs rgyas kyi mtshan 
4a5(dkar chag): bstan pa'i le'u/'phags pa'i bden pa'i le'u/de bzhin gshegs pa'o 'od zer las sangs rgyas pa'i le'u/byang chub sems dpa'i dris pa snang ba'i le'u/spyod yul yongs su dag 
4a6(dkar chag): pa'i le'u/bzang po'i dpal gyi le'u/ri rab rtse mor gshegs pa'i le'u/byang chub sem dpa'i tshogs kyi tshigs su bcang ba'i le'u/rnam pa dgong pa bcu bstan pa'i le'u/kha par 
4a7(dkar chag): tshangs par spyod pa'i le'u/sems dang po bskyed pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po bsags pa'i tshigs bcad kyi le'u/chos snang ba'i le'u/mtsho ba'i gnas rnam 'phrul gyi le'u/tshigs 
4b1(dkar chag): su bcad pa bstan pa'i le'u/yon tan me tog bsags pa'i le'u/gter mi zad pa bcu bstan pa'i le'u/de bzhin gshegs pa dga' ldan du bzhugs pa'i le'u/dga' ldan du byang 
4b2(dkar chag): sems bsdus pa'i tshigs bcad kyis le'u/byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyis yongs su bsngo ba'i le'u las/bam po sum cu rtsa gnyis pa nas bzhi bcu rtsa gcig yan chad//
4b3(dkar chag): ga par rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyis bsngo ba'i le'u las gong gi 'phros bam po bzhi bcu rtsa gnyis pa nas/bzhi bcu rtsa bzhi pa yan/sa bcu pa'i le'u/byang sems kun tu bzang po'i spyod 
4b4(dkar chag): pa bstan pa'i le'u/ting 'dzin bcu'i le'u/mngon par shes pa'i le'u/bzod pa'i le'u/sems kyi rgyal pos dris    nas grangs la 'jug pa bstan pa'i le'u/tshe'i tshad kyi le'u
4b5(dkar chag): byang chub sems dpa'i gnas kyi le'u/da par sangs rgyas kyi chos bsam gyi mi khyab pa bstan pa'i le'u/de bzhi gshegs pa'i sku'i mtshan rgya mtsho bstan pa'i le'u/dpe byad bzang po'i 'od 
4b6(dkar chag): phreng bstan pa'i le'u/kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa bstan pa'i le'u/de gzhin gshegs pa skye ba 'byung ba bstan pa'i le'u/'jig rten las 'das pa'i le'u las bam po bdun cu rtsa drug pa
4b7(dkar chag): nas brgyad cu rtsa bzhi pa yan/cabar 'jig rten las 'das pa'i la'u las gong gi 'phros pam po gcig dang sdong pos brgyan pa'i le'u las bam po brgyad cu rtsa drug pa nas brgya yan/
5a1(dkar chag): @#//cha bar gong gi 'phros bam po brgya gcig nas brgya bcu bzhi pas rdzogs pa yan////mdo sde'i gras/kA par 'phags pa bskal pa bzang po/
5a2(dkar chag): kha bar rgya che rol pa/'jam dpal rnam par rol pa/'jam dpal rnams par 'phrul pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin rlabs sems can la gzigs shing sangs 
5a3(dkar chag): rgyas kyi zhing gi bkod pa kun tu ston pa/ga par ye shes rgyas pa'i mdo sde rin po che/mtha' yas pa mthar phyin pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul la 'jug pa ye shes snang ba'i 
5a4(dkar chag): rgyan gyi mdo//nga par dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su 'dzin pa'i mdo//zung gi mdo'i chos kyi rnam grangs/khye'u snang ba bsam gyi mi khyab pas bstan pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs/de bzhin 
5a5(dkar chag): gshegs pa rnams kyi zhing gi yon tan brjod pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs/dkyil 'khor brgyad pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs/ca bar dgongs pa nges 'grel/lang kar gshegs 
5a6(dkar chag): pa'i mdo ji snyed pa/lang kar gshegs pa rin po che'i mdo las/sangs rgyas thams cad gyi gsung gi snying po'i le'u/ga ya go ri'i mdo//cha par rgyan stug po bkod pa ji snyed 
5a7(dkar chag): pa|snying rje chen po padam dkar po//snying rje pad dkar/ja bar dam chos pad dkar/chos thams cad kyi yon tan bkod pa'i rgyal po//bde ba can gyi bkod pa/za ma tog bkod pa/
5b1(dkar chag): dkon mchog gi za ma tog/rin po che'i mtha' zhes bya ba/nya bar yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa chen po las lha dang mi la sogs pas zhus pa'i le'u/'da' dka' ye shes/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 
5b2(dkar chag): mdzod ces bya ba/dkon mchog 'byung gnas/gser gyi mdo dang /gser gyi bye ma lta bu'i mdo//ta par ting 'dzin rgyal po'i mdo//chos nyid rang gi ngo bo las mig yo ba'i mdo//rab tu 
5b3(dkar chag): zhi ba rnam par nges pa'i cho 'phrul ting nge 'dzin/sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi phyag rgya'i ting nge 'dzin/dpa' bar 'gro ba'i ting nge 'dzin/tha par 
5b4(dkar chag): da ltar gyi sangs rgyas mngon sum du bzhugs pa'i ting nge 'dzin/bsod nams thams cad sdud pa'i ting nge 'dzin/khye'u bzhi'i ting nge 'dzin/ting 'dzin mchog dam pa/'dus pa rin po che'i 
5b5(dkar chag): tog gi bzungs/rdo rje snying po'i bzungs/sgo mtha' yas par sgrub pa'i bzungs/sgo drug pa'i bzungs/da par rnam par mi rtog pa la 'jug pa'i bzungs/tshigs su bcad pa gnyis pa'i 
5b6(dkar chag): bzungs/theg pa chen po la dad pa rab tu sgom pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul thabs kyis yul la rnam par 'phrul pa bstan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po nges par bstan 
5b7(dkar chag): pa bzungs kyi dbang phyug rgyal pos zhus pa/nam mkha' mdzod kyis zhus pa/byams pas zhus pa'i mdo//spyan ras gzigs kyis zhus pa chos bdun pa/spobs pa'i blo gros kyis zhus pa 
6a1(dkar chag): @#//na par blo gros rgya mtshos zhus pa/klu'i rgyal po rgya mtshos zhus pa che 'bring chung gsum/klu'i rgyal po ma dros pas zhus pa/mi 'am ci'i rgyal po lngan pas 
6a2(dkar chag): zhus pa/ba bar tshangs pas zhus pa/tshangs byin gyis zhus pa/tshangs pa khyad par sems kyis zhus pa/lha'i bu rab rtsal sems kyis zhus pa/dpal dbyig gis zhus pa/
6a3(dkar chag): rin chen dra bas zhus pa/rin chen zla bas zhus pa/bde byed kyis zhus pa/yul 'khor skyong gis zhus pa/rnam par 'phrul pa'i rgyal pos zhus pa/lha mo dri med 'od kyis zhus pa/
6a4(dkar chag): theg chen man ngag bu mo rin chen gyis zhus pa/bram ze mo dpal ldan gyis zhus pa/bgres mos zhus pa/pha bar 'jam dpal gyis dris pa/bdag med pas dris pa/'jig 
6a5(dkar chag): rten 'dzin gyis dris pa/blo gros mi zad pas bstan pa/li tsA ba'i dri ma med par grags pas stan pa/'jam dpal gyis bstan pa/byang chub kyi phyogs bstan pa/kun rdzob dang 
6a6(dkar chag): don dam pa'i bden pa bstan pa/chos thams cad 'byung ba med par bstan pa/ba bar sbyin sogs phar phyin lnga bstan pa/sbyin pa'i phar phyin bstan pa/sbyin pa'i phan yon 
6a7(dkar chag): bstan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bstan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan dang ye shes bsam gyi mi khyab pa'i yul la 'jug par bstan pa/sangs rgyas kyi stobs 
6b1(dkar chag): bskyed pa'i cho 'phrul rnam par 'phrul ba bstan pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos bsam gyi se mi khyab par bstan pa/mar me mdzad kyis lung bstan pa/tshangs pa'i dpal lung bstan pa/bud med 
6b2(dkar chag): 'gyur ba lung bstan pa/bu mo zla mchog lung bstan pa/bde ldan ma lung bstan pa/lha mo chen mo dpal lung bstan pa/rgyal ba'i blo gros zhes bya ba/spyan ras gzigs kyi mdo//
6b3(dkar chag): 'jam dpal gnas pa/byams pa bdud rtsi brjod pa/byams pa 'jug pa/chos kyi rjes su mthun par 'jug pa/ma/bar dad pa'i stobs bskyed pa la 'jug pa'i phyag rgya/dwa ba 
6b4(dkar chag): dang ma nges pa las 'gro ba'i phyag rgya la 'jug pa/chos kyi phyag rgya/mar me 'bul ba/grong khyer gyi mtsho ba/zas kyi mtsho ba rnam par dag pa/glang po che'i rtsal lta bu'i mdo//
6b5(dkar chag): sgra chen seng ge'i sgra bsgrags pa/sa lu ljang ba'i mdo//rtan cing 'brel par 'byung ba dang po dang /rnam par dbye ba bstan pa/rten cing 'brel par 'byung pa'i mdo//sor mo'i 
6b6(dkar chag): 'phreng ba la phan pa'i mdo//rgyal po gzugs can snying po la gdams pa/bad sa'i rgyal po 'char byed la gdams pa/ma skyes dgra'i 'gyod pa bsal ba/dpal sbas kyi 
6b7(dkar chag): mdo//las grib rgyal bar dag pa las sgrib rgyun gcod bag tsa bar sangs rgyas kyi sde snod tshul khrims 'chal ba tshar gcod pa/rgyal po gsal rgyal la gdams pa/rnga bo che 
7a1(dkar chag): @#//chen po'i mdo//sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i le'u/lhag bsam bstan pa'i mdo//gsum la skyabs su 'gro ba'i mdo//srid pa 'pho ba/rnam par 'thag pa thams cad 
7a2(dkar chag): bsdud pa/sangs rgyas bgro ba/de bzhin gshegs pa bgro ba/'dus pa chen po'i mdo las de bzhin gshegs pa'i dpal gyi dam tshig/tsha bar dkon cog sprin/sprin chen po'i 
7a3(dkar chag): mdo//sprin chen po las phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems dpa' dpal rgya mtsho 'dus pa'i dga' ston chen po la rnam par brtse ba'i le'u/sprin chen po rlung gi dkyil 'khor gyi le'u/sprin 
7a4(dkar chag): chen po rlung gi dkyil 'khaur las char dbab pa'i le'u cho ga dang bcas pa/bcom ldan 'das kyi gtsug tor chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung ba bsgrub pa/bdud kyi nyi 
7a5(dkar chag): tshe 'byung ba/dza bar chos yang dag par bsdud pa/sa snying 'khor lo bcu pa/phyir mi ldog pa'i 'khor lo//ting 'dzin 'khor lo'i mdo//yongs su bsngo ba'i 'khor lo//wa 
7a6(dkar chag): bar chos kyi tshul/chos kyi phud po/don dam pa'i chos kyi rnam par rgyal ba/chos dang don rnam par 'byed pa/byang chub sems dpa'i so thar chos bzhi sgrub pa/chos bzhi bstan 
7a7(dkar chag): pa/chos bzhi bstan pa sh+lau ka bcu pa/chos bzhi mi gtong ba/bzhi sgrub pa/chos gsum pa'i mdo//chos kyi rgyal mtshan gyi mdo//chos kyi rgya mtsho//chos kyi rgyal mo mdo 
7b1(dkar chag): sde nyi ma'i snying po//de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po'i mdo//yi ge med pa'i za ma tog/rnam par snang mdzad kyi snying po//nam mkha'i snying po'i mdo//thabs la mkhas pa'i mdo//zha par sangs 
7b2(dkar chag): rgyas gyi mtshan lnga stong bzhi brgya lnga bcu rtsa gsum pa/yang dag par sbyong tshul nam mkha'i mngag gi 'dul ba'i bzod pa bstan pa/thar pa chen po phyogs su rgyas pa/'gyod tshang gi 
7b3(dkar chag): sdig pa sbyang ba ste sangs rgyas yongs su sgrub pa rnam par bkod pa/'phags pa rtog pa chen po yongs su rgyas pa'i mdo las phyag 'tshal cho ga mtshan nas brjod pa'i gleng gzhi/me tog gi tshogs 
7b4(dkar chag): kyi mdo//za bar spang spong phag rgya pa/bsam gyi mi khyab pa'i rgyal po//phyogs bcu'i mun sil/sangs rgyas bdun pa/sangs rgyas brgyad pa/sangs rgyas bcu pa/
7b5(dkar chag): sangs rgyas bcu gnyis pa/sangs rgyas kyi dbu rgyan/sangs rgyas mi spong ba/sangs rgyas kyi sa/dkyil 'khor brgyad pa/bkra shis brgyad pa/dkon mchog gsum rjes su dran pa'i mdo//
7b6(dkar chag): bslab pa gsum gyi mdo//sku gsum gyi mdo//phung po gsum gyi mdo//bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i bsngo ba/'gro ba thams cad yongs su skyob par byed pa'i bsngo ba/mdo dran 
7b7(dkar chag): pa nyer bzhag/'a par gong gi 'phros bam po zha lnga yan/ya par bam po zhe drug nas 'thab bral gyi lha rnams kyi sa las rtag tu bde ba'i sa ste brgyad pa yan/ra bar gong gi 
8a1(dkar chag): 'phros dran pa nyer bzhag ji tsam 'gyur ba rdzogs pa yan/mdo chen po sgyu ma'i dra ba/mdo chen po gzugs can snying pos bsu ba/mdo chen po stong pa nyid/
8a2(dkar chag): mdo chen po stong pa nyid chen po//mdo rgyal mtshan mchog/mdo rgyal mtshan dam pa/mdo chen po lnga gsum pa/rdo 'phangs pa'i mdo//gzhon nu dpe'i mdo//khams mang po'i mdo/
8a3(dkar chag): gaN+Di'i mdo//gaN+Di'i dus kyi mdo//dge ba'i bshes gnyen sten pa'i mdo//la bar mngon par 'byung ba'i mdo//dge slong la rab tu gces pa'i mdo//tshul khrims yang dag par ldan 
8a4(dkar chag): pa'i mdo//ltung ba sde lnga'i dge mi dge'i 'bras bu bstan pa'i mdo//mchog tu gdags pa'i mdo//rnam grol lam las sbyangs pa'i yon tan bstan pa'i mdo//tshe'i mtha'i mdo//'chi 
8a5(dkar chag): 'pho ba ji ltar 'gyur ba bstan pa'i mdo//mi rtag pa nyid kyi mdo//yang mi rtag pa nyid kyi mdo/'du shes bcu bstan pa/'du shes bcu gcig bstan pa/yangs pa'i grong khyer du 
8a6(dkar chag): 'jug pa'i mdo//mtshan mo bzang po'i mdo//mun pa'i nags tshal gyi sgo'i mdo//pha ma'i mdo//bden bzhi'i mdo//don rnam par nges pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs/don 
8a7(dkar chag): rgyas pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs rmad du byung ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs brnyan bzhag pa'i phan yon yang dag pa bstan pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs
8b1(dkar chag): mchod rten bskor ba'i tshigs bcad/gsal rgyal gyis tshigs su bcad pa/tshigs su bcad pa gcig pa/tshigs su bcad pa bzhi pa/klu'i rgyal po rnga sgra'i tshigs su bcad pa/mched du brjod pa'i 
8b2(dkar chag): tshoms/skyes bu dam pa'i mdo//dga' bo rab tu byung ba'i mdo//lha'i mdo//lha'i mdo nyung du/zla ba'i mdo//khang bu brtsegs pa'i mdo//gnas 'jog gi mdo//dga' ba can gyi mdo//
8b3(dkar chag): 'khar sil gyi mdo//'khar sil 'chang ba'i kun tu spyod pa ston pa'i cho ga/chos kyi 'khor lo'i mdo//las kyi rnam smin gyi 'bras bu bstan pa'i mdo//las rnam 'byed/rnam 'byed chung ba las rnam 
8b4(dkar chag): par 'gyur ba/sha bar mdo sde las brgya pa bam po nyer drug yan dang sdom gyi tshigs su bcad pa bdun pa'i gtam rgyud dang po yan/sa par gong gi 'phros yongs rdzogs dang /'dzangs blun/
8b5(dkar chag): kun tu rgyu sen ring gis zhus pa/byams pa'i mdo//byams pa lung bstan pa/ha bar gang po la sogs pa'i rtogs pa brjod pa/sangs rgyas gyi rtogs brjod shes ldan gyi mdo//phag mo'i rtogs 
8b6(dkar chag): brjod/ma ga d+ha bzang mo'i rtogs brjod/a par bsod nams stobs kyi rtogs pa brjod pa/zla 'od kyi rtogs brjod/dpal gyi sde'i rtogs brjod/gser mdog gi sngon gyi sbyor ba bstan pa/
8b7(dkar chag): rgyal bu don 'grub kyi mdo//thams cad sgrol gyi skyes rabs mdo tshangs pa'i dra ba/thabs mkhas pa chen po sangs rgyas drin lan bkab pa/legs nyes kyi rgyu 'bras bstan pa/dge mi dge'i 
9a1(dkar chag): @#//las kyi rnam smin bstan pa/zla ba'i snying pos zhus pa'i mdo las bstan pa'i gnas 'jig lung bstan pa/ri glang ru lung bstan pa/rta ga sna'i rtogs rjod/mig 
9a2(dkar chag): bcu gnyis pa/chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba'i mdo//skyes rab kyi gleng gzhi'i mdo//lcang lo can gyi pho brang gi mdo//'dus pa chen po'i mdo//byams pa'i mdo//byams pa

9a4(dkar chag): po'i mdo//nyi ma dang zla ba'i mdo//bkra shis chen po'i mdo bcas rdzogs pa yan//kipar gtsug tor dpa' bar 'gro ba//myang 'das kapar ngam po nyer brgyad yan/khapar gong gi

9a7(dkar chag): sbyor ba'i rtsa rgyud/de'i rgyud phyi ma'i phyi ma/kye rdor rtsa rgyud brtag pa gnyis pa/rdo rje gur zhes bya ba/de'i rgyud phyi ma phyag chen thig le/phyi ma'i phyi ma yes snying po//bshad 
9b1(dkar chag): rgyud ye shes thig le/'bras rgyud de kho na nyid/kha bar bde mchog rgyud 'bum las nyung dur grags pa/bde mchog nges brjod rgyud bla ma/bde mchog sdom 'byung gi rgyud/dpal rdo rje

10a1(dkar chag): @#//bshad rgyud drag po gsum 'dul/gos sngon can gyi cho ba rdo rje be con gyi rgyud/rdo rje gdengs pa'i rgyud/khams gsum rnam rgyal/btum po gsang ba'i rgyud/de'i
